    if (!window.CM.Tools.cookie.get('eg_cookieconsent_status') && window.localStorage) {
      var count = window.CM.Tools.localStorage.get('pageviews_count');
      count = count ? parseInt(count, 10)+1 : 1;

      if (count > 3) {
        window.CM.Tools.cookie.set('eg_cookieconsent_status', 'dismiss', 31000000);
      } else {
        window.CM.Tools.localStorage.set('pageviews_count', count);
      }
    }

      projectId: 'weebot',
      userConsent: window.CM.Tools.cookie.get('eg_cookieconsent_status') ? true : false
    });

    cmAgent.onGetCart = function(cart) {
      console.log('onGetCart called', cart);
      var cmItems = [];
      if (cart.item_count > 0) {
        cart.items.forEach(function(item) {
          var cmItem = {
            externalId: ''+item.id,
            name: item.title && item.title != '' ? ''+item.title : ''+item.sku,
            quantity: parseInt(item.quantity, 10),
            price: parseFloat(item.final_price) / 100,
            brand: ''+item.vendor,
          };
          if (item.sku && item.sku != '') cmItem.sku = ''+item.sku;
          if (item.variant_id && item.variant_id != '') cmItem.variant = ''+item.variant_id;
          cmItems.push(cmItem);
        });
      }
      cmAgent.setCart({items: cmItems});
      cmAgent.dispatch();
